Build only, no GPU job: the user asked to build this image now and to
decide whether to serve it after the cap-128 run 39114949128 returns.
The ASPIRE build-only selector is the supported CPU-only build route;
it materializes the Firmus archive as well and skips every GPU workflow.

Contents beyond the image of 39110496019 (823b79f):
- add-rmsnorm-group-quant.patch restored (e8b9817): the sources show the
  same formulas and rounding points as flashinfer + per_token_group_quant;
  the GPU check keeps residual bitwise, scale within 1 BF16 ulp and every
  dequantized value within one E4M3 step and only records how many FP8
  codes differ.
- sm90-flat-nvlink-barriers.patch (af144db, rebased onto all 14 patches,
  all geometries): per-CTA epoch release-adds replace the pre-combine and
  cleanup grid syncs, no pre-pull barrier; expected Decode -4 to -7 us per
  call; correctness argument in
  research/runtime-performance/2026-10-18-megamoe-flat-nvlink-barriers.md.
- SwapAB selection up to 144 tokens (host patch
  patches/deep-gemm-host/sm90-swap-ab-144.patch, header guard
  sm90-swap-ab-geometry-guard.patch, _C.so rebuilt in the image by
  containers/rebuild-deepgemm-host.py, recorded in the build JSON and
  checked by check-firmus-image.sh): lets a later run raise the Decode cap
  above 32 per rank without falling to the non-SwapAB path that cost run
  39110496019 its Decode time.
DeepGEMM series: 16 patches, header 5d8e4ce3; timeline patch regenerated.

This commit itself sets the branch's runtime default back to Decode cap
128 (graph bs up to 32), the setting under test in 39114949128; the cap
for any serving run of this image is chosen per run.

Validation: all 16 DeepGEMM patches apply to v0.1.5, all 23 SGLang
patches apply on fc275674 and the five source checks pass, pytest, ruff,
bash -n, actionlint and the tag check pass. First exercise of the _C.so
rebuild inside the Buildah chroot; a failure stops the build there.
